What is the Governor vetoing?

Of 2,456 bills that reached the Governor in the 2025–26 session, 211 were vetoed (8.6%) and 2,245 were signed. This page shows which topics, directions and authors the vetoes fall on.

211vetoed
2,245became law
8.6%veto rate
193vetoed after a two-thirds vote in both houses
Direction compares each bill with current California law, as everywhere else on this site. The Governor’s veto messages (the stated reasons) are not part of this data; each bill shows its own summary and votes.

Where the Governor lands

Each marker is the average direction of the bills the Governor signed or vetoed on that topic, measured against current law. The dark tick is the average of all bills on the topic that reached the Governor. Restrictive is −0.25 or lower, Permissive is +0.25 or higher, otherwise Balanced. Pick an action, then click a topic to see the bills behind it.
